Sub FURALL2()
' make uravn for FUR2_ur(#fur, #contur)
Dim I_in(10), I_out(10) As String
Dim KF_c(10), kf_r(10) As Single
Dim F_name(14) As String
Dim Fm_gas(14) As String
Dim UVZ(14) As String
Dim F_c(10) As String
Dim T_c(10) As String
Dim T_co(10) As String
Dim r_con(10) As Single
Dim K_kf(10) As Single
Application.ScreenUpdating = True
For i = 1 To 14
For j = 1 To 10
FUR2_ur(i, j) = ""
Next j
Next i
Worksheets("CODE").Cells(528, 34).Value = ""
K_gaz = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(438, 34).Value

For fi = 452 To 522 Step 7 'цикл по печам
For i = 1 To 10
I_in(i) = ""
I_out(i) = ""
KF_c(i) = 0
kf_r(i) = 0
r_con(i) = 0
K_kf(i) = 0
F_c(i) = ""
T_c(i) = ""
T_co(i) = ""
Next i

fi2 = (fi - 445) / 7
F_name(fi2) = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(fi, 40).Value
If Len(F_name(fi2)) > 1 Then ' условие длинны имени печи 2

Fm_gas(fi2) = Replace("0.28*GTc" + F_name(fi2), " ", "") ' старая формулировка до блокировки расчётноо компонента при затухании
Fm_gas(fi2) = Replace("0.28*F_FUR2[" + Str(fi2) + "]*GTc" + F_name(fi2), " ", "") ' новая формулировка с затуханием
'Fm_gas(fi2) = Replace("GTc" + F_name(fi2), " ", "")
UVZ(fi2) = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(fi + 1, 42).Value
If IsNumeric(UVZ(fi2)) = True Then UVZ(fi2) = " 1 "
If UVZ(fi2) = "" Then UVZ(fi2) = " 1 "
t0 = Replace("T0_" + F_name(fi2), " ", "")
t1 = Replace("T1_" + F_name(fi2), " ", "")
Tkr = Replace("Tkr_" + F_name(fi2), " ", "")
p1 = Replace("P1_" + F_name(fi2), " ", "")
p2 = Replace("P2_" + F_name(fi2), " ", "")
qk = Replace("Qk_" + F_name(fi2), " ", "")
qr = Replace("Qr_" + F_name(fi2), " ", "")
t3 = Replace("T3_" + F_name(fi2), " ", "")
kig = Replace("KIG_" + F_name(fi2), " ", "")
' идем на лист чтоб отметить печь
 Worksheets(F_name(fi2)).Cells(4, 32).Value = 1
 Worksheets(F_name(fi2)).Cells(4, 33).Value = UVZ(fi2)
    chK = 0
    zn = "e27"
    For ci = 1 To 6 'цикл по конутрам (сбор)
        I_in(ci) = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(fi + 1, ci + 33).Value
        If IsNumeric(I_in(ci)) = True Then I_in(ci) = ""
        I_out(ci) = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(fi + 2, ci + 33).Value
        If IsNumeric(I_out(ci)) = True Then I_out(ci) = ""
        KF_c(ci) = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(fi + 3, ci + 33).Value
        If IsNumeric(KF_c(ci)) = False Then KF_c(ci) = 0
        kf_r(ci) = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(fi + 4, ci + 33).Value
        If IsNumeric(kf_r(ci)) = False Then kf_r(ci) = 0
        
        If Len(I_in(ci)) * Len(I_out(ci)) * KF_c(ci) * kf_r(ci) > 0 Then 'считаем контур печи, он действителен
            chK = chK + 1
            r_con(ci) = 1
            F_c(ci) = Replace("0.28*Fc" + I_in(ci) + I_out(ci), " ", "")
            'F_c(ci) = Replace("Fc" + I_in(ci) + I_out(ci), " ", "")
            T_c(ci) = Replace("Tc" + I_in(ci), " ", "")
            T_co(ci) = Replace("Tc" + I_out(ci), " ", "")
            K_kf(ci) = Format(Log(KF_c(ci) + kf_r(ci)) * 1000, 0#) / 1000
            zn = zn + "+(2000 - " + T_c(ci) + ") * Math.Pow(" + F_c(ci) + "+1, 0.2) * " + Str(K_kf(ci))
        End If 'считаем контур печи, он действителен
    Next ci 'цикл по конутрам (сбор)
    
    For ci = 1 To 6 'цикл по конутрам (печать)
        If r_con(ci) > 0 Then



     
         


        '    XX0[12] = FcI_12I_14 * XX0[11];
        '    XX0[13] = aa0 * aa1 * 1200*(1+17.3 * Math.Pow(z8 * z9, 0.001));
         '   XX0[14] = XX0[12] / 12400 + 0.5 * (1 + XX0[12] / XX0[13]);
        '    XX0[15] = XX0[13] / 12400 + 0.5 * (1 + XX0[13] / XX0[12]);
        '    XX0[14] = 1 / (e27 + XX0[14]);
        '    XX0[15] = 1 / (e27 + XX0[15]);
         '   if (XX0[14] > 1 - e27) XX0[14] = 1 - e27;
         '   if (XX0[15] > 1 - e27) XX0[15] = 1 - e27;
        '    XX0[17] =  (1 - XX0[14]) * TcI_13 + XX0[14] * T0_P_1;
        '    XX0[16] = (1 - XX0[15]) * T0_P_1 + XX0[15] * TcI_13;
        '    TcI_15 = (TcI_15 * 50 + XX0[17]) / 51;
        
        
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = " // Расчёт печи " + F_name(fi2) + " для контура №" + Str(ci) + ". Нагрев источника " + I_in(ci) + " до источника " + I_out(ci) + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + " if (" + kig + "<0.01+e27) F_FUR2[" + Str(fi2) + "]=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + " if (" + kig + ">1-e27) F_FUR2[" + Str(fi2) + "]=1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "aa1=e27+" + Str(K_gaz) + "*Math.Pow(" + kig + ",0.1)*0.1*" + Fm_gas(fi2) + "* Math.Pow(" + UVZ(fi2) + ",0.1); // газ общий в печи на сжигание" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "aa2=e27+(2000-" + T_c(ci) + ")*Math.Pow(" + F_c(ci) + "+1,0.2)*" + Str(K_kf(ci)) + "; // долевой коэффициент теплосъема текущего контура" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "aa0=aa2/(" + zn + "); // доля 0...1 текущего контура во всём теплообмене печи" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + t0 + "=(Tc_vozd*0.01+100*" + t0 + "+1490*Math.Pow(" + UVZ(fi2) + ",0.001)+10*" + F_name(fi2) + "+500*Math.Pow(" + UVZ(fi2) + ",0.1))/101.01; // температура горения в топке" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        'S_FUR2[" + Str(fi2) + "]

        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "XX0[11]=(2200*(1-U6" + I_in(ci) + ")+4260*U6" + I_in(ci) + ")*(0.963+0.001*" + T_c(ci) + "); // теплоемкость сырья без учета испарения" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        em1 = "Em" + I_in(ci)
        em2 = "Em" + I_out(ci)

        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + " if (" + T_co(ci) + "<" + T_c(ci) + ") " + T_co(ci) + "=" + T_c(ci) + "+e27 *" + UVZ(fi2) + ";" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "// уравнение то Будника" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "XX0[12] = e27+" + F_c(ci) + " * XX0[11]; // W hol!  Вт" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "XX0[13] = e27+aa0 * aa1 * 1200*(1+17.3 * Math.Pow(" + UVZ(fi2) + ", 0.001)); // W gor!  Вт" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "XX0[14] = XX0[12] / " + Str(KF_c(ci) + kf_r(ci)) + "/S_FUR2[" + Str(fi2) + "] + 0.5 * (1 + XX0[12] / XX0[13]); // 1/x hol!" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "XX0[15] = XX0[13] / " + Str(KF_c(ci) + kf_r(ci)) + "/S_FUR2[" + Str(fi2) + "] + 0.5 * (1 + XX0[13] / XX0[12]); // 1/x gor!" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "XX0[15] = 1 / (e27 + XX0[15]); // x gor!" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "if (XX0[14] > 0.97) XX0[14] = 0.97; // nx hol!" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "XX0[17] =  (1 - XX0[14]) * " + T_c(ci) + " + XX0[14] * " + t0 + "; // температура выхода сырья целевая" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
  
        at_sr = "0.03*" + t0 + "+0.97*" + T_c(ci)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "if (XX0[15] > 0.97) XX0[17]=" + T_c(ci) + "+(" + t0 + "-(" + at_sr + "))*XX0[13]/XX0[12];// температура выхода сырья целевая при сверх высокой S м2" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "if (XX0[15] > 0.97) XX0[15] = 0.97; // nx gor!" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "XX0[16] = (1 - XX0[15]) * " + t0 + " + XX0[15] * " + T_c(ci) + "; // температура выхода газов целевая" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "XX0[17] =  (XX0[17]  * " + Replace("Fc" + I_in(ci) + I_out(ci), " ", "") + " + Tc_vozd *" + Str(0.00015 * (KF_c(ci) + kf_r(ci))) + ")/( " + Replace("Fc" + I_in(ci) + I_out(ci), " ", "") + " + " + Str(0.00015 * (KF_c(ci) + kf_r(ci))) + "); // температура выхода сырья с УЧЕТОМ ТЕПЛОПОТЕРЬ" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "XX0[16] =  (XX0[16]  * 17*aa0*" + Replace("GTc" + F_name(fi2), " ", "") + " + Tc_vozd *" + Str(0.00015 * (KF_c(ci) + kf_r(ci))) + ")/( 17*aa0*" + Replace("GTc" + F_name(fi2), " ", "") + " + " + Str(0.00015 * (KF_c(ci) + kf_r(ci))) + "); // температура выхода газов с УЧЕТОМ ТЕПЛОПОТЕРЬ" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + " if (XX0[16]>0.55*" + t0 + ") XX0[16] =0.55*" + t0 + "; // температура выхода газов с УЧЕТОМ ТЕПЛОПОТЕРЬ-ограниченная" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + p2 + "=" + F_name(fi2) + "*0.99;// P газов после радиации" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "XX0[18]=XX0[16]*4*" + Str(kf_r(ci)) + "/(4*" + Str(kf_r(ci)) + "+" + Str(KF_c(ci)) + "); // Q радиации локальное" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + qr + "=(50*" + qr + "+(XX0[18]/1000*aa0+(1-aa0)*" + qr + "))/51; // Q радиации общее кВт" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + "XX0[18]=XX0[16]*" + Str(KF_c(ci)) + "/(4*" + Str(kf_r(ci)) + "+" + Str(KF_c(ci)) + "); // Q конвекции локальное" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) = FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + qk + "=(50*" + qk + "+(XX0[18]/1000*aa0+(1-aa0)*" + qk + "))/51; // Q конвекции общее кВт" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        End If
        
        If Len(FUR2_ur(fi2, ci)) > 0 Then
        Worksheets("CODE").Cells(528, 34).Value = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(528, 34).Value + FUR2_ur(fi2, ci) + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        End If
    Next ci 'цикл по конутрам (печать)
End If ' условие длинны имени печи 2
Next fi 'цикл по печам
End Sub

Sub FURALL()

Application.ScreenUpdating = True
For i = 1 To 38
FUR_ur(i) = ""
Next i
Cells(419, 36).Value = ""

For i = 379 To 416
imPe = Cells(i, 35).Value
If Len(imPe) > 1 Then
Tp0 = "T0_" + imPe
Tp1 = "T1_" + imPe
Tp3 = "T3_" + imPe
Tkr = "Tkr_" + imPe
p1 = "P1_" + imPe
p2 = "P2_" + imPe

KFk = "(" + Str(Cells(i, 38).Value) + "*S_FUR[" + Str(i - 378) + "])"

Tf2 = "Tc" + Cells(i, 37).Value

em2 = "Em" + Cells(i, 37).Value

cim = c1 + "*(1-1.48*" + em1 + "+1.36*" + em2 + ")"
UVZ = Cells(i, 40).Value
osh = 0
If VarType(UVZ) = vbEmpty Then osh = 1
If VarType(UVZ) = vbNull Then osh = 1
If VarType(UVZ) = vbInteger Then osh = 1
If VarType(UVZ) = vbDouble Then osh = 1

If osh = 1 Then
MsgBox ("Для печи " + imPe + " указано уравнение имеющее отличный от строчного формат. Необходимо привести запись к строчному формату!")
End If

' расчёт расходов

Call CODE_GEN3
Worksheets(ima).Activate
Call CODE_GEN3

For j0 = 5 To 131 Step 7

If aa = imGor Then Fa_txt = Worksheets(ima).Cells(j0 + 4, 17).Value
Next j0
If Len(Fg_txt) * Len(Fa_txt) < 1 Then

Sub all_kms()
ufProgress.Show (0)
If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(5, 58).Value < 2 Then Application.ScreenUpdating = False
aa = Worksheets.Count
'проверка имен параметров связи
For i = 1 To aa
If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(5, 58).Value < 2 Then Application.ScreenUpdating = False
bb = ActiveWorkbook.Sheets(i).Name
If Not bb = "CODE" Then
If Not bb = "EX" Then
Worksheets(bb).Activate
Call list_kms
End If
End If
Call PrPro(100 * (i - 2) / (aa - 2), "Расчёт коэффициентов местных сопротивлений!")
Next i
Application.ScreenUpdating = True
Worksheets("CODE").Activate
If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(1, 1).Value = 0 Then
Else
MsgBox ("ВЫПОЛНЕНО!")
End If
Unload ufProgress
End Sub

Sub list_kms()
okms = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(50, 174).Value
For i = 5 To 131 Step 7
df = Cells(i, 7).Value
df2 = Len(df)

If Len(Cells(i, 7).Value) > 1 Then
df3 = Len(Cells(i, 7).Value)
Cells(i + 3, 13).Value = Format(100000 * okms / (0.000001 + Cells(i + 3, 10).Value), 0#) / 100

If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(26, 57).Value = 1 Then
Cells(i + 3, 26).Value = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(25, 59).Value
End If

End If
Next i
End Sub

Sub all_GLW()
answer = MsgBox("Присвоить всем высотным отметкам аппаратов и их штуцеров данные на основе выводов G/L/W?" + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + "Будут заменены текущие данные по высотным отметкам!", vbQuestion + vbYesNo + vbDefaultButton2, "Присвоение высотных отметок!")
If answer = vbYes Then
Application.ScreenUpdating = False
For i = 3 To Worksheets.Count
Load ufProgress
ufProgress.Show (0)
' высота
If Len(Worksheets(i).Cells(9, 4).Value) < 1 Then Worksheets(i).Cells(9, 4).Value = 0.5
If IsNumeric(Worksheets(i).Cells(9, 4).Value) = False Then
Worksheets(i).Cells(9, 4).Value = 3
Else
If Worksheets(i).Cells(9, 4).Value < 0.5 Then Worksheets(i).Cells(9, 4).Value = 0.5
End If
' отметка
If Len(Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 4).Value) < 1 Then Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 4).Value = 0.1
If IsNumeric(Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 4).Value) = False Then
Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 4).Value = 0.1
Else
If Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 4).Value < -5 Then Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 4).Value = 0
End If
For j = 5 To 131 Step 7
' штуцера
If Len(Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value) < 1 Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value = 0.05
If IsNumeric(Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value) = False Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value = 0.05
If Len(Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value) < 1 Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value = 0.05
If IsNumeric(Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value) = False Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value = 0.05
If (Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 3).Value) = 0 Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value = 0.05
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "G" Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value = 99.5
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "g" Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value = 99.5

If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "L" Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value = 0.5
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "l" Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value = 0.5

If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "W" Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value = 0.001
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "w" Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value = 0.001

oi_p = 0
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "G" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "g" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "w" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "W" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "L" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "l" Then oi_p = 1
If oi_p = 1 Then Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 3).Value = 1
If oi_p = 0 Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = ""

Next j
Call PrPro(100 * (i - 2) / (Worksheets.Count - 2), "Присвоение значений высотным отметкам и высотам!")
Next i
Application.ScreenUpdating = True
End If
Unload ufProgress
End Sub

Sub all_GS_count()
Load ufProgress
ufProgress.Show (0)
aa = Worksheets.Count
If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(5, 58).Value < 2 Then Application.ScreenUpdating = False
Call all_kms
If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(5, 58).Value < 2 Then Application.ScreenUpdating = False
LAMA = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(8, 67).Value
aa = Worksheets.Count
'проверка имен параметров связи
For i = 1 To aa
Call PrPro(100 * (i - 2) / (Worksheets.Count - 2), "Расчёт гидравлики!")
If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(5, 58).Value < 2 Then Application.ScreenUpdating = False
bb = ActiveWorkbook.Sheets(i).Name
If Not bb = "CODE" Then
If Not bb = "EX" Then
Worksheets(bb).Activate
If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(5, 58).Value < 2 Then Application.ScreenUpdating = False
Call all_GS
If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(5, 58).Value < 2 Then Application.ScreenUpdating = False
End If
End If
Next i
Application.ScreenUpdating = True
Worksheets("CODE").Activate
Unload ufProgress
If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(1, 1).Value = 0 Then
Else
MsgBox ("ВЫПОЛНЕНО!")
Worksheets("CODE").Cells(11, 69).Value = 0.181
Call Massh_KMS_Rlin
End If
End Sub

answer = MsgBox("Уделить все существующие данные о диаметрах и длинах?" + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + "Записываем единые D и L!", vbQuestion + vbYesNo + vbDefaultButton2, "Доступно дополнение.")

If answer = vbYes Then

LL = ""
dd = ""
If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(5, 58).Value < 2 Then Application.ScreenUpdating = False
aa = Worksheets.Count
'проверка имен параметров связи
For i = 1 To aa
If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(5, 58).Value < 2 Then Application.ScreenUpdating = False
bb = ActiveWorkbook.Sheets(i).Name
If Not bb = "CODE" Then
If Not bb = "EX" Then


For j = 5 To 131 Step 7
If Len(Worksheets(bb).Cells(j, 7).Value) > 1 Then
Worksheets(bb).Cells(j + 3, 10).Value = dd
Worksheets(bb).Cells(j + 3, 11).Value = LL
End If
Next j

End If
End If
Next i
Application.ScreenUpdating = True
Worksheets("CODE").Activate
MsgBox ("ВЫПОЛНЕНО!")
End If
End Sub

Sub H_otm_plus()
shg_h = Cells(4, 73).Value
For i = 3 To Worksheets.Count
Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 4).Value = Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 4).Value + shg_h
Next i
End Sub
Sub H_otm_minus()
shg_h = Cells(4, 73).Value
For i = 3 To Worksheets.Count
Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 4).Value = Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 4).Value - shg_h
Next i
End Sub

Sub Voi_X()
d02 = InputBox("Введите устанавливаемый объем", "Работа с объемом ОИ!")

d02 = Replace(d02, ".", ",")
If IsNumeric(d02) = False Then d02 = 10


answer = MsgBox("Присвоить всем OI источникам объем " + Str(d02) + " м3?" + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + "", vbQuestion + vbYesNo + vbDefaultButton2, "Задание единых объемов OI")

If answer = vbYes Then

    aa = Worksheets.Count
    
    For i = 3 To aa
        ind0 = 0
        For j = 5 To 131 Step 7
            ind = 0
            If Len(Worksheets(i).Cells(j, 7).Value) > 1 Then
                If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "W" Then ind = 1
                If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "G" Then ind = 2
                If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "L" Then ind = 3
                If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "w" Then ind = 1
                If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "g" Then ind = 2
                If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "l" Then ind = 3
                If Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 3).Value = 1 Then ind = 4
                
                If ind > 0 Then
                ind0 = 1
                End If
            End If
        Next j
        
        If ind0 > 0 Then
        Worksheets(i).Cells(7, 4).Value = d02
        End If
    Next i
End If
End Sub


Sub Voi_10()
answer = MsgBox("Присвоить всем OI источникам объем 10 м3?" + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + "", vbQuestion + vbYesNo + vbDefaultButton2, "Задание единых объемов OI")

If answer = vbYes Then

    aa = Worksheets.Count
    
    For i = 3 To aa
        ind0 = 0
        For j = 5 To 131 Step 7
            ind = 0
            If Len(Worksheets(i).Cells(j, 7).Value) > 1 Then
                If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "W" Then ind = 1
                If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "G" Then ind = 2
                If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "L" Then ind = 3
                If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "w" Then ind = 1
                If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "g" Then ind = 2
                If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "l" Then ind = 3
                If Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 3).Value = 1 Then ind = 4
                
                If ind > 0 Then
                ind0 = 1
                End If
            End If
        Next j
        
        If ind0 > 0 Then
        Worksheets(i).Cells(7, 4).Value = 10
        End If
    Next i
End If
End Sub
Sub STUC_not_OI()
Application.ScreenUpdating = False
ufProgress.Show (0)
aa = Worksheets.Count
For i = 3 To aa
Call PrPro(100 * (i - 2) / (aa - 2), "Правка штуцеров для OI и не OI!")

tip_ist = Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 3).Value * Worksheets(i).Cells(9, 3).Value
If tip_ist = 0 Then ' не ОИ
Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 3).Value = 0
Worksheets(i).Cells(9, 3).Value = 0
Worksheets(i).Cells(13, 3).Value = 0
Worksheets(i).Cells(9, 4).Value = 0.1
For j = 5 To 131 Step 7
ima_stu = Worksheets(i).Cells(j, 7).Value
If Len(ima_stu) > 1 Then
Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value = 60

oi_p = 0
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "G" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "g" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "w" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "W" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "L" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "l" Then oi_p = 1
If oi_p = 0 Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = ""





End If
Next j
Else ' ОИ

For j = 5 To 131 Step 7
ima_stu = Worksheets(i).Cells(j, 7).Value
If Len(ima_stu) > 1 Then
VISH = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value
If Cells(9, 72).Value = 1 Then
If VISH > 99.7 Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value = "99,7"
If VISH < 0.3 Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value = "0,31"
End If
oi_p = 0
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "G" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "g" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "w" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "W" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "L" Then oi_p = 1
If Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = "l" Then oi_p = 1
If oi_p = 0 Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value = ""


End If
Next j


End If
Next i
Unload ufProgress
Application.ScreenUpdating = True
End Sub

Sub Give_FC() ' дать имена FC всем расходам
For i = 3 To Worksheets.Count
IMA_i = Worksheets(i).Cells(4, 4).Value
For j = 5 To 131 Step 7
ima_s = Worksheets(i).Cells(j, 7).Value
If Len(ima_s) > 1 Then Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 20).Value = Replace("Fc" + ima_s + IMA_i, " ", "")
Next j
Next i

End Sub

Sub vv1_test()
aa = Worksheets.Count
For i = 1 To aa
bb = ActiveWorkbook.Sheets(i).Name
If Not bb = "CODE" Then
If Not bb = "EX" Then
On Error Resume Next
If Not Worksheets(i).Cells(8, 28).Interior.Color = Worksheets("EX").Cells(8, 28).Interior.Color Then
Worksheets("EX").Cells(8, 28).Copy
Worksheets(i).Activate
For o = 8 To 71 Step 7
aaa = Worksheets(i).Cells(o, 28).Value
Worksheets(i).Paste Cells(o, 28)
Worksheets(i).Cells(o, 28).Value = aaa
Worksheets(i).Cells(o, 29) = Worksheets("EX").Cells(8, 29)
Next o
End If
Worksheets(i).Cells(2, 8) = Worksheets("EX").Cells(2, 8)
End If
End If
Next i
Worksheets("CODE").Activate
End Sub

Sub vvv() ' конвертация наши их и их наши
d = 1
If d = 1 Then
 'Cells(6, 4).Value = Replace(Cells(6, 4).Value, "+1*1", "+1")
 Application.DisplayAlerts = False
Dim myData As New DataObject
myData.SetText (Cells(6, 14))
'Перемещаем текст из DataObject в буфер обмена
myData.PutInClipboard
'Проверяем содержимое буфера обмена
ActiveSheet.Paste Range("A60:A1000")
Application.DisplayAlerts = True
Txt11 = Cells(6, 4).Value
Txt12 = Cells(6, 14).Value
Range(Cells(60, 1), Cells(1000, 1)).Interior.Color = Cells(1, 1).Interior.Color
Range(Cells(60, 1), Cells(1000, 1)).Replace "double ", ""
Range(Cells(60, 1), Cells(1000, 1)).Replace "=0;", ""
Cells(6, 4).Value = Txt11
Cells(6, 14).Value = Txt12
a34 = 0
For i = 60 To 1000
If Not Cells(i, 1).Value = "" Then
If i - 54 = 61 Then a34 = 150
Cells(i - 54 + a34, 29).Value = Cells(i, 1).Value
End If
Next i
Range(Cells(60, 1), Cells(1000, 1)).ClearContents
End If
Cells(6, 32).Value = ""
Cells(6, 40).Value = ""
a34 = 0
For i = 6 To 500
If i = 61 Then
a34 = 50
End If
If Not Cells(i + a34, 30).Value = "" Then
If Not Cells(i + a34, 29).Value = "" Then
On Error Resume Next
Cells(6, 32).Value = Cells(6, 32).Value + Cells(i + a34, 29).Value + " = V(""" + Cells(i + a34, 30).Value + """);" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Cells(6, 40).Value = Cells(6, 40).Value + "V(""" + Cells(i + a34, 30).Value + """," + Cells(i + a34, 29).Value + ");" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
End If
End If
Next i
End Sub
Sub del_vvv()
If Worksheets("CODE").Cells(5, 58).Value < 2 Then Application.ScreenUpdating = False
For i = 6 To 1500
If i = 61 Then
a34 = 150
End If

On Error Resume Next
Cells(i + a34, 29).ClearContents
Cells(i + a34, 30).ClearContents
Next i
Application.ScreenUpdating = True
End Sub


Sub f_vig()

fn = Application.GetSaveAsFilename(InitialFileName:=IMA_f("_DATA_TXT") + ".txt", FileFilter:=", *.txt")
If fn = "False" Then
        MsgBox "Ничего не выбрано!"
        Exit Sub
        End If


aa = Worksheets.Count
If aa < 3 Then Exit Sub

Dim data101(2300, 150, 50) As String
For i = 3 To aa

 data101(i, 0, 1) = Worksheets(i).Tab.ColorIndex
 data101(i, 0, 2) = Worksheets(i).Name
                data101(i, 4, 4) = Worksheets(i).Cells(4, 4).Value
                data101(i, 123, 1) = Worksheets(i).Cells(123, 1).Value
                data101(i, 4, 3) = Worksheets(i).Cells(4, 3).Value
                data101(i, 7, 4) = Worksheets(i).Cells(7, 4).Value
                data101(i, 9, 4) = Worksheets(i).Cells(9, 4).Value
                data101(i, 9, 3) = Worksheets(i).Cells(9, 3).Value
                data101(i, 13, 3) = Worksheets(i).Cells(13, 3).Value
                data101(i, 11, 4) = Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 4).Value
                data101(i, 11, 3) = Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 3).Value
                data101(i, 16, 3) = Worksheets(i).Cells(16, 3).Value
                data101(i, 17, 3) = Worksheets(i).Cells(17, 3).Value
            
                
                For j = 5 To 131 Step 7 '''''''''''''''''''''''''''    КОПИРУЕМ ДАННЫЕ ТАБЛИЦ НА ЛИСТЕ ИСТОЧНИКА
                
                If Len(Worksheets(i).Cells(j, 7).Value) > 1 Then

For j2 = 9 To 28
For j3 = 1 To 4
data101(i, j - 1 + j3, j2) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j - 1 + j3, j2).Value
Next j3
Next j2

               
                

                
                
                data101(i, j + 6, 5) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 6, 5).Value
                If data101(i, j + 6, 5) = 2 Then
                Else
                data101(i, j + 6, 5) = 1
                End If
                
                data101(i, j + 4, 5) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value
                data101(i, j, 7) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j, 7).Value
                data101(i, j + 6, 5) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 6, 5).Value
                data101(i, j + 3, 7) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 7).Value
                data101(i, j + 4, 7) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 7).Value
                data101(i, j + 3, 17) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 17).Value
                data101(i, j + 3, 20) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 20).Value
                data101(i, j + 3, 26) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 26).Value
                data101(i, j + 3, 28) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 28).Value
                data101(i, j + 4, 26) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 26).Value
                data101(i, j + 5, 7) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value ' ВЫВОД ФАЗЫ
                data101(i, j + 6, 7) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 6, 7).Value  ' % уровня допустимого для выхода потока
                data101(i, j - 1, 6) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j - 1, 6).Value
                data101(i, j + 3, 12) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 12).Value
                'data101(i, j + 3, 7) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 7).Font.Color  ' цвет источника

                If j = 5 Then
                data101(i, 1, 1) = Worksheets(i).Cells(1, 1).Value
                data101(i, 4, 4) = Worksheets(i).Cells(4, 4).Value
                End If
              
                End If

                Next j



Next i

Open fn For Output As #1
Print #1, "# "
Print #1, "# DATA_" + Cells(23, 62).Value + "_EXPORTS ;"
Print #1, "# "
Print #1, "# "

'data101(2300, 150, 50)
 'data101(i, 0, 1) = Worksheets(i).Tab.ColorIndex
 'data101(i, 0, 2) = Worksheets(i).Name
 aa11 = data101(3, 8, 7)
For i1 = 3 To aa
Print #1, "listy_" + Str(i1) + " # " + data101(i1, 0, 1) + " # " + data101(i1, 0, 2)
For i2 = 1 To 150
line_101 = ""
For i3 = 1 To 50
line_101 = line_101 + data101(i1, i2, i3) + " # "
Next i3
Print #1, line_101
Next i2
Next i1


Close #1
MsgBox ("DONE!")
End Sub

Sub f_vig2()

Dim sImportFile2 As String, sFile As String
    Dim sThisBk As Workbook
    Dim impSh As Worksheet
    Dim vfilename As Variant
    Dim oNewRow As ListRow
    Dim svod As ListObjects

MsgBox ("Выберите файл эимпорта для архивации данных о листах связи!")
sImportFile2 = Application.GetOpenFilename( _
    FileFilter:="Microsoft Excel Workbooks, *.xlsm; *.xlsb; *.xls; *.xlsx")
 
    If sImportFile2 = "False" Then
        MsgBox "Ничего не выбрано!"
        Unload ufProgress
        Exit Sub
        End If



        vfilename = Split(sImportFile2, "\")
        sFile = vfilename(UBound(vfilename))
        Application.Workbooks.Open Filename:=sImportFile2
                'открываем файл по ссылке и лист с указанным названием
        Set wbBk = Workbooks(sFile)




idetif = "_DATA_TXT001"
kl = wbBk.FullName
kl1 = wbBk.Name
k12 = Replace(kl1, ".xlsm", "")
k12 = Replace(k12, ".xlsb", "")
k12 = Replace(k12, "Utilite_1", "")
fn_SHORT = k12
k12 = Left(k12, Len(k12) - 12) + ")"
kl2 = k12 + "_" + idetif
kl2 = Replace(kl2, " ", "")
kl2 = Replace(kl2, "GDC", "_")
DIR_my = Replace(kl, kl1, "")
kl0 = Replace(kl, kl1, "") + kl2
s1 = Day(Date)
S10 = Replace(Str(s1), " ", "")
If s1 < 10 Then S10 = Replace("0" + Str(s1), " ", "")
s2 = Month(Date)
S20 = Replace(Str(s2), " ", "")
If s2 < 10 Then S20 = Replace("0" + Str(s2), " ", "")
s3 = Year(Date)
S30 = Replace(Str(s3), " ", "")
If s3 < 10 Then S30 = Replace("0" + Str(s3), " ", "")
s4 = Hour(Time)
S40 = Replace(Str(s4), " ", "")
If s4 < 10 Then S40 = Replace("0" + Str(s4), " ", "")
s5 = Minute(Time)
S50 = Replace(Str(s5), " ", "")
If s5 < 10 Then S50 = Replace("0" + Str(s5), " ", "")
kl0 = kl0 + "_[" + S10 + "." + S20 + "." + S30 + " " + S40 + ".." + S50 + "] "
FOLD_my = Replace(kl0, DIR_my, "")
IMA_f001 = kl0



fn = Application.GetSaveAsFilename(InitialFileName:=IMA_f001 + ".txt", FileFilter:=", *.txt")
If fn = "False" Then
        MsgBox "Ничего не выбрано!"
        Exit Sub
        End If


aa = wbBk.Worksheets.Count
If aa < 3 Then Exit Sub

Dim data101(2300, 150, 50) As String
For i = 3 To aa

 data101(i, 0, 1) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Tab.ColorIndex
 data101(i, 0, 2) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Name
                data101(i, 4, 4) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(4, 4).Value
                data101(i, 123, 1) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(123, 1).Value
                data101(i, 4, 3) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(4, 3).Value
                data101(i, 7, 4) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(7, 4).Value
                data101(i, 9, 4) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(9, 4).Value
                data101(i, 9, 3) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(9, 3).Value
                data101(i, 13, 3) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(13, 3).Value
                data101(i, 11, 4) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 4).Value
                data101(i, 11, 3) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(11, 3).Value
                data101(i, 16, 3) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(16, 3).Value
                data101(i, 17, 3) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(17, 3).Value
            
                
                For j = 5 To 131 Step 7 '''''''''''''''''''''''''''    КОПИРУЕМ ДАННЫЕ ТАБЛИЦ НА ЛИСТЕ ИСТОЧНИКА
                
                If Len(wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j, 7).Value) > 1 Then

For j2 = 9 To 28
For j3 = 1 To 4
data101(i, j - 1 + j3, j2) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j - 1 + j3, j2).Value
Next j3
Next j2

               
                

                
                
                data101(i, j + 6, 5) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 6, 5).Value
                If data101(i, j + 6, 5) = 2 Then
                Else
                data101(i, j + 6, 5) = 1
                End If
                
                data101(i, j + 4, 5) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 5).Value
                data101(i, j, 7) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j, 7).Value
                data101(i, j + 6, 5) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 6, 5).Value
                data101(i, j + 3, 7) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 7).Value
                data101(i, j + 4, 7) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 7).Value
                data101(i, j + 3, 17) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 17).Value
                data101(i, j + 3, 20) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 20).Value
                data101(i, j + 3, 26) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 26).Value
                data101(i, j + 3, 28) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 28).Value
                data101(i, j + 4, 26) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 4, 26).Value
                data101(i, j + 5, 7) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 5, 7).Value ' ВЫВОД ФАЗЫ
                data101(i, j + 6, 7) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 6, 7).Value  ' % уровня допустимого для выхода потока
                data101(i, j - 1, 6) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j - 1, 6).Value
                data101(i, j + 3, 12) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 12).Value
                'data101(i, j + 3, 7) = Worksheets(i).Cells(j + 3, 7).Font.Color  ' цвет источника

                If j = 5 Then
                data101(i, 1, 1) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(1, 1).Value
                data101(i, 4, 4) = wbBk.Worksheets(i).Cells(4, 4).Value
                End If
              
                End If

                Next j



Next i

wbBk.Close SaveChanges:=False

Open fn For Output As #1
Print #1, "# "
Print #1, "# DATA_" + Cells(23, 62).Value + "_EXPORTS ;"
Print #1, "# "
Print #1, "# "

'data101(2300, 150, 50)
 'data101(i, 0, 1) = Worksheets(i).Tab.ColorIndex
 'data101(i, 0, 2) = Worksheets(i).Name
 aa11 = data101(3, 8, 7)
For i1 = 3 To aa
Print #1, "listy_" + Str(i1) + " # " + data101(i1, 0, 1) + " # " + data101(i1, 0, 2)
For i2 = 1 To 150
line_101 = ""

Next i3
Print #1, line_101
Next i2
Next i1


Close #1

End Sub


Sub clap_r()
aa0 = Cells(67, 6).Value ' параметр
aa1 = Cells(68, 6).Value ' клапан
b1 = Cells(69, 6).Value
b2 = Cells(70, 6).Value
Cells(73, 6).Value = ""
Cells(73, 6).Value = Cells(73, 6).Value + "// " + aa0 + " для " + aa1 + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Cells(73, 6).Value = Cells(73, 6).Value + "if (factory.isAuto(form, """ + aa1 + """))" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Cells(73, 6).Value = Cells(73, 6).Value + "{ " + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Cells(73, 6).Value = Cells(73, 6).Value + "aa2 = (" + Str(b1) + "); // направление работы клапана" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Cells(73, 6).Value = Cells(73, 6).Value + "RP_1 = " + Str(b2) + "; // шаг уровня мягкого регулирования" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Cells(73, 6).Value = Cells(73, 6).Value + "RP_2 = 5; //  предел различия в % отн. для перехода между уровнями регулирования" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Cells(73, 6).Value = Cells(73, 6).Value + "RP_3 = RP_1*6; // шаг уровня грубово регулирования" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Cells(73, 6).Value = Cells(73, 6).Value + "xcc1 =  V(""" + aa0 + """); // регулируемый параметр" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Cells(73, 6).Value = Cells(73, 6).Value + "xcc2=factory.GetAbs(form, " + aa1 + ");" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Cells(73, 6).Value = Cells(73, 6).Value + "if (Math.Abs((xcc2 - xcc1)/ xcc2) > RP_2) RP_1 = RP_3; RP_4 = 1; if ((xcc2 - xcc1) * (xcc1 - xcc3) > 0) RP_4 = 0.1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
Cells(73, 6).Value = Cells(73, 6).Value + "aa1 = ((xcc2 - xcc1)/ xcc2 * (aa2) * (RP_1)*(RP_4) + V(""" + aa1 + """)); // алгоритм регулирования клапана" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

Cells(73, 6).Value = Cells(73, 6).Value + "if(aa1 < 0) aa1 = 0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

Sub Set_K_to_preset()
For i = 86 To 169
If Len(Cells(i, 19).Value) > 2 Then
imaN = Cells(i, 29).Value
j = 63
Do
If Cells(j, 54).Value = imaN Then Cells(j, 55).Value = Cells(i, 16).Value
j = j + 1
Loop While Len(Cells(j, 54).Value) > 1
End If
Next i
End Sub

Sub Set_LD_to_list()

        d_pp = Cells(88, 10).Value
        osh = 0
        If d_pp > 1200 Then osh = 1
        If d_pp < 20 Then osh = 1
        If osh = 1 Then MsgBox ("Ошибка с уставкой диаметра по умолчанию!")
        
        l_pp = Cells(88, 11).Value
        osh = 0
        If l_pp > 2 Then osh = 1
        If l_pp < 0.02 Then osh = 1
        If osh = 1 Then MsgBox ("Ошибка с уставкой длины участка по умолчанию!")
        
For i = 86 To 169
    If Len(Cells(i, 19).Value) > 2 Then
        
        imaPO = Cells(i, 27).Value
        imaPI = Cells(i, 28).Value
        imaPOP = Cells(i, 29).Value
        imaPIP = Cells(i, 30).Value
        
        n_po = 0
        n_pi = 0
        For j = 5 To 131 Step 7
            imapop_0 = Worksheets(imaPO).Cells(j, 7).Value
            If Len(imapop_0) > 2 Then
            If Replace(imapop_0, " ", "") = Replace(imaPOP, " ", "") Then n_po = j
            End If
            
            imapip_0 = Worksheets(imaPI).Cells(j, 7).Value
            If Len(imapip_0) > 2 Then
            If Replace(imapip_0, " ", "") = Replace(imaPIP, " ", "") Then n_pi = j
            End If
        Next j
        
        If n_po = 0 Then MsgBox ("Не найдено соответсвие POP-точки для источника " + imaPO)
        If n_pi = 0 Then MsgBox ("Не найдено соответсвие PIP-точки для источника " + imaPI)
        
        ' простановка диаметров и длинн
        If n_po > 0 Then
          Worksheets(imaPO).Cells(n_po + 3, 10).Value = d_pp
          Worksheets(imaPO).Cells(n_po + 3, 11).Value = l_pp
        End If
        If n_pi > 0 Then
          Worksheets(imaPI).Cells(n_pi + 3, 10).Value = d_pp
          Worksheets(imaPI).Cells(n_pi + 3, 11).Value = l_pp
        End If
    
    End If
Next i
End Sub

Function Rep_ur(URAVN As String)
a1 = Replace(URAVN, " ", "")
a1 = Replace(a1, "*", "^")
a1 = Replace(a1, ")", "^")
a1 = Replace(a1, "+", "^")
a1 = Replace(a1, "-", "^")
a1 = Replace(a1, "/", "^")
a1 = Replace(a1, ".", "^")
a1 = Replace(a1, ",", "^")
a1 = Replace(a1, "^^", "^")
Rep_ur = a1
End Function

Sub Load_lib_comps() ' загрузка компонентов с библиотеки

Dim lib_name(42) As String
For i = 1 To 42
lib_name(i) = Cells(i + 6, 275).Value
Next i


For g_i = 1 To 6
For s_i = 1 To 4
st_i = 238 + g_i * 7 + s_i
ima_co = Cells(st_i, 3).Value

del_co = 0
If Len(ima_co) < 2 Then del_co = 1
If ima_co = "Empty" Then del_co = 1
If del_co = 1 Then
Cells(st_i, 5).Value = 0
For k = 1 To 8
Cells(st_i, 5 + k).Value = ""
Next k
End If

sovp = 0
For j = 1 To 42
If ima_co = lib_name(j) Then sovp = j + 6
Next j

If sovp > 0 Then
For k = 1 To 8
Cells(st_i, 5 + k).Value = Cells(sovp, 276 + k).Value
Next k
End If

Next s_i
Next g_i
MsgBox ("Поиск в библиотеке компонентов завершен!" + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + "Не забудьте проставить составы каждой группы!!!")

End Sub




Sub Load_comp_6_4()
' расчёт средних
Dim xy(4) As Single
For i = 1 To 6
summ_0 = 1E-17
For j = 1 To 4
xy(j) = Cells(7 * i + 239 - 1 + j, 5).Value
summ_0 = summ_0 + xy(j)
Next j
For j = 1 To 4
xy(j) = xy(j) / summ_0
Cells(7 * i + 239 - 1 + j, 5).Value = Format(xy(j) * 100000, 0#) / 100000
Next j
For j = 6 To 13
summ_0 = 0
For i2 = 1 To 4
aa1 = Cells(7 * i + 239 - 1 + i2, j).Value
summ_0 = summ_0 + aa1 * xy(i2)
Next i2
Cells(7 * i + 239 - 1 + 5, j).Value = Format(summ_0 * 100, 0#) / 100
Next j
Next i

'сгружаем в составы 6к
For i = 1 To 6
i1 = 20 + i
i2 = 243 + i * 7
For j = 1 To 8
j1 = 73 + j
j2 = 5 + j
Cells(i1, j1).Value = Cells(i2, j2).Value
Next j

' имена
n_00 = 0
aa2 = ""
For i3 = 1 To 4
If Len(Cells(i2 - 5 + i3, 3).Value) > 1 Then
If n_00 = 1 Then aa2 = aa2 + "+"
aa2 = aa2 + Cells(i2 - 5 + i3, 3).Value
n_00 = 1
End If
Next i3
Cells(i1, 72).Value = aa2
Next i

MsgBox ("DONE!")
End Sub

Sub prov_DS()
Cells(119, 11).Value = ""
codOH_u = ""
Dim dops(100) As Single
For i = 1 To 100
dops(i) = 0
Next i

aa = Worksheets.Count
' проверка уравнений
For i = 118 To 168
a1 = Len(Rep_ur((Cells(i, 6).Value)))
a2 = Len(Rep_ur((Cells(i, 7).Value)))
a3 = Len(Rep_ur((Cells(i, 8).Value)))
a4 = Len(Rep_ur((Cells(i, 9).Value)))

a0 = a1 * a2 * (a3 + a4)

If a0 < 1 Then
If a1 + a2 + a3 + a4 > 0 Then codOH_u = codOH_u + " Ошибка строки ДУДР №" + Str(i - 117) + "! Не полная запись строки! " + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
End If
Next i


' проверяем источники
For i = 118 To 168
a1 = Cells(i, 6).Value
a2 = Cells(i, 7).Value
If Len(a1 + a2) * Len(a1) * Len(a2) > 3 Then
' значит заполнено оба источника и надо проверить их имена
For II0 = 3 To aa
bb = ActiveWorkbook.Sheets(II0).Name
If a1 = bb Then dops(i - 117) = dops(i - 117) + 1 ' значения накопителя по каждой строке ( тут если меньше 2-х то что то с источниками не так)
If a2 = bb Then dops(i - 117) = dops(i - 117) + 10
Next II0
If dops(i - 117) < 11 Then
If dops(i - 117) = 10 Then codOH_u = codOH_u + " Ошибка источника ДУДР №" + Str(i - 117) + "! Источник <" + Cells(i, 6).Value + "> отсутсвует в перечне библиотеки! " + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
If dops(i - 117) = 1 Then codOH_u = codOH_u + " Ошибка источника ДУДР №" + Str(i - 117) + "! Источник <" + Cells(i, 7).Value + "> отсутсвует в перечне библиотеки! " + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
If dops(i - 117) = 0 Then codOH_u = codOH_u + " Ошибка источника ДУДР №" + Str(i - 117) + "! Источники <" + Cells(i, 7).Value + "> и  <" + Cells(i, 6).Value + "> отсутсвуют в перечне библиотеки! " + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
End If
End If
Next i



' составляю массив имен задвижек
Dim imKr(13000) As String
For i = 1 To 3000
imKr(i) = ""
Next i
chK = 0
i3 = 63
Do

' проверяем уравнения задвижек
For i = 118 To 168
a1 = Cells(i, 8).Value
a2 = Cells(i, 9).Value
a1 = Rep_ur((a1))
a2 = Rep_ur((a2))

' запускаем внутренний цикл для строки по выявлению совпадений по каждой задвижке
    If Len(a1) > 1 Then
    ' уравнение 1
    stop_i = 1
    wa1 = a1 + "^"
    Do
    iw1 = InStr(wa1, "^")
    n_t = Left(wa1, iw1 - 1) ' выделенное по очереди имя задвижки
    wa1 = Right(wa1, Len(wa1) - iw1)
    If Len(Replace(wa1, "^", "")) < 1 Then stop_i = 0
    
    srav = 0
    ' цикл сравнения
    For zad = 1 To chK
    If Replace(n_t, " ", "") = Replace(imKr(zad), " ", "") Then srav = srav + 1
    Next zad
    If srav = 0 Then codOH_u = codOH_u + " Ошибка уравнения №1 ДУДР №" + Str(i - 117) + "! Указана задвижка <" + n_t + "> отсуствующая в перечне библиотеки." + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
    If srav > 1 Then codOH_u = codOH_u + " Ошибка уравнения №1 ДУДР №" + Str(i - 117) + "! Указана задвижка <" + n_t + "> встречается в библиотеке более 1-го раза." + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
    If srav = 0 Then dops(i - 117) = dops(i - 117) - 0.1
    If srav > 1 Then dops(i - 117) = dops(i - 117) - 0.2
    
    Loop While stop_i = 1
    End If
    
    ' уравнение 2
    If Len(a2) > 1 Then
    stop_i = 1
    wa2 = a2 + "^"
    Do
    iw2 = InStr(wa2, "^")
    n_t = Left(wa2, iw2 - 1) ' выделенное по очереди имя задвижки
    wa2 = Right(wa1, Len(wa2) - iw2)
    If Len(Replace(wa2, "^", "")) < 1 Then stop_i = 0
    
    srav = 0
    ' цикл сравнения
    For zad = 1 To chK
    If Replace(n_t, " ", "") = Replace(imKr(zad), " ", "") Then srav = srav + 1
    Next zad
    If srav = 0 Then codOH_u = codOH_u + " Ошибка уравнения №2 ДУДР №" + Str(i - 117) + "! Указана задвижка <" + n_t + "> отсуствующая в перечне библиотеки." + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
    If srav > 1 Then codOH_u = codOH_u + " Ошибка уравнения №2 ДУДР №" + Str(i - 117) + "! Указана задвижка <" + n_t + "> встречается в библиотеке более 1-го раза." + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
    If srav = 0 Then dops(i - 117) = dops(i - 117) - 0.1
    If srav > 1 Then dops(i - 117) = dops(i - 117) - 0.2
    Loop While stop_i = 1
    End If

Next i

ccc = ActiveSheet.Index

If Not bb = "CODE" Then
If Not bb = "EX" Then
ActiveSheet.Name = Cells(4, 4).Value
End If
End If

ccb = ActiveSheet.Name
'проверка имен параметров связи
For i = 1 To aa
bb = ActiveWorkbook.Sheets(i).Name
If Not bb = "CODE" Then
If Not bb = "EX" Then

GG = Worksheets(i - 1).Name

GG = Worksheets(i).Name

GG = Worksheets(i).Cells(4, 4).Value
nam(i) = Worksheets(i).Cells(4, 4).Value
End If
End If
Next i
For i = 1 To aa - 1
For j = i + 1 To aa
If nam(i) = nam(j) Then
nam(j) = nam(j) + "_1"
Worksheets(j).Cells(4, 4).Value = nam(j)
End If
Next j
Next i
For i = 1 To aa
bb = ActiveWorkbook.Sheets(i).Name
If Not bb = "CODE" Then
If Not bb = "EX" Then
Worksheets(i).Activate
ActiveWorkbook.Sheets(i).Name = Cells(4, 4).Value
'Call CODE_GEN
End If
End If
Next i
Application.ScreenUpdating = True
Worksheets("EX").Copy After:=Worksheets(ccc)

Worksheets(as_0 + 1).Name = dfr
Worksheets(as_0 + 1).Range("D4").Select

Worksheets("EX").Cells(1, 1).Value = dfr
End Sub

'MsgBox ("точка 4 " + Str(i) + " " + Str(j) + " " + ima2)

For i20 = 1 To ch_sv

'MsgBox ("точка 6 " + Str(i) + " " + Str(i2) + " " + Str(j) + " " + Str(ch_sv) + " " + ima2 + " " + list_sv(i2))
Next i20
End If
If sovp = 0 Then
ch_sv = ch_sv + 1
list_sv(ch_sv) = Replace(ima2 + ima1, " ", "")
End If
End If
Next j
Next i

For i = 1 To ch_sv
ch_doub = ch_doub + 1
i_doub(ch_doub) = "double " + Replace("Fc" + list_sv(i) + "", " ", "") + "=0;"
ch_doub = ch_doub + 1
i_doub(ch_doub) = "double " + Replace("Pr_Fc" + list_sv(i) + "", " ", "") + "=0;"
ch_doub = ch_doub + 1
i_doub(ch_doub) = "double " + Replace("Fv" + list_sv(i) + "", " ", "") + "=0;"
ch_doub = ch_doub + 1
i_doub(ch_doub) = "double " + Replace("Kv" + list_sv(i) + "", " ", "") + "=0;"
ch_doub = ch_doub + 1
i_doub(ch_doub) = "double " + Replace("ro" + list_sv(i) + "", " ", "") + "=0;"
Call PrPro(100 * (i) / (ch_sv + 0.0001), "Формируем double для связей.")
Next i



' Ликвидация дублей

Dim n_Dub(200000) As String

dub_ch = 0

End If

Next i

Next i

Close #3
MsgBox ("Найдено для double " + Str(dub_ch) + " дублей! Всего строк для double: " + Str(n_ch) + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + "Файл с дублями сохранен:" + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + fn13)
End If


' возврат новособранных дабл в старый массив и число

Next i

    a1 = InStr(ish21, "//")

    ish22 = Replace(ish21, " ", "")
    Else
    a2 = Left(ish21, a1 - 1) ' левая часть
    a3 = Right(ish21, Len(ish21) - a1 + 1) ' правая часть
    s = s
    ish22 = Replace(a2, " ", "") + a3
    End If

cht = InStr(a45, ".")

h1 = Mid(a45, cht - 1, 2)
h2 = Mid(a45, cht, 2)
h01 = Replace(h1, ".", "")
h02 = Replace(h2, ".", "")
AS1 = 0
AS2 = 0
If IsNumeric(h01) = True Then
AS1 = 1
End If
If IsNumeric(h02) = True Then
AS2 = 1
End If

If AS2 = 1 Then
If AS1 = 0 Then a45 = Replace(a45, h1 + h02, h01 + "0" + "___" + h02)
If AS1 = 1 Then a45 = Replace(a45, h1 + h02, h01 + "___" + h02)
End If
End If
Loop Until cht = 0
a45 = Replace(a45, "___", ".")


Print #10, a45
End If
Loop

End Sub

Function KLUCH_V_LOG(KLUCH_nom As String)
text_temp = ""
KLUCH_nom = Replace(KLUCH_nom, "=", "")
ima_fu = Replace(KLUCH_nom + "=", " ", "")
KLUCH_nom = Replace(KLUCH_nom, " ", "")
len_kl = Str(1 + Len(KLUCH_nom))
                text_temp = text_temp + "XX0[8]:=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "aa1:=Length(main.bph.param9.ValS.replace(' ',''));" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "TEMP_STRING:=main.bph.param9.ValS.replace(' ','').replace('" + ima_fu + "','');" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "aa2:=Length(TEMP_STRING);" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "if (aa1-aa2=" + len_kl + ") then XX0[8]:=XX0[8]+1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "if (aa2>0) then XX0[8]:=XX0[8]+1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "if (XX0[8]=2) then Val(TEMP_STRING, " + KLUCH_nom + ");" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "XX0[8]:=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "aa1:=Length(main.bph.param10.ValS.replace(' ',''));" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "TEMP_STRING:=main.bph.param10.ValS.replace(' ','').replace('" + ima_fu + "','');" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "aa2:=Length(TEMP_STRING);" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "if (aa1-aa2=" + len_kl + ") then XX0[8]:=XX0[8]+1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "if (aa2>0) then XX0[8]:=XX0[8]+1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "if (XX0[8]=2) then Val(TEMP_STRING, " + KLUCH_nom + ");" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
KLUCH_V_LOG = text_temp
End Function

Function KLUCH_V_LOG2(KLUCH_nom As String, KLUCH_max As Single, KLUCH_min As Single, KLUCH_koeff As Single)
text_temp = ""
KLUCH_nom = Replace(KLUCH_nom, "=", "")
ima_fu = Replace(KLUCH_nom + "=", " ", "")
KLUCH_nom = Replace(KLUCH_nom, " ", "")
len_kl = Str(1 + Len(KLUCH_nom))
                text_temp = text_temp + "XX0[8]:=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "aa1:=Length(main.bph.param9.ValS.replace(' ',''));" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "TEMP_STRING:=main.bph.param9.ValS.replace(' ','').replace('" + ima_fu + "','');" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "aa2:=Length(TEMP_STRING);" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "if (aa1-aa2=" + len_kl + ") then XX0[8]:=XX0[8]+1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "if (aa2>0) then XX0[8]:=XX0[8]+1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "if (XX0[8]=2) then Val(TEMP_STRING, " + KLUCH_nom + ");" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "XX0[8]:=0;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "aa1:=Length(main.bph.param10.ValS.replace(' ',''));" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "TEMP_STRING:=main.bph.param10.ValS.replace(' ','').replace('" + ima_fu + "','');" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "aa2:=Length(TEMP_STRING);" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "if (aa1-aa2=" + len_kl + ") then XX0[8]:=XX0[8]+1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "if (aa2>0) then XX0[8]:=XX0[8]+1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "if (XX0[8]=2) then Val(TEMP_STRING, " + KLUCH_nom + ");" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                ' ввод ограничений и делителя
                text_temp = text_temp + KLUCH_nom + ":=" + KLUCH_nom + "/(0+" + Str(KLUCH_koeff) + ");" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                
                t_k_min = Str(KLUCH_min)
                t_k_max = Str(KLUCH_max)
                If Abs(KLUCH_min) < 1 Then
                t_k_min = Replace("0" + Str(KLUCH_min), " ", "")
                If KLUCH_min < 0 Then t_k_min = Replace("-" + t_k_min, " ", "")
                End If
                If Abs(KLUCH_max) < 1 Then
                t_k_max = Replace("0" + Str(KLUCH_max), " ", "")
                If KLUCH_max < 0 Then t_k_max = Replace("-" + t_k_max, " ", "")
                End If
                text_temp = text_temp + "if (" + KLUCH_nom + ">" + t_k_max + ") then " + KLUCH_nom + ":=" + t_k_max + ";" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                text_temp = text_temp + "if (" + KLUCH_nom + "<" + t_k_min + ") then " + KLUCH_nom + ":=" + t_k_min + ";" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                
                
KLUCH_V_LOG2 = text_temp
End Function

'KLUCH7 = "K_tau"

Dim ttr As String
        ttr = "//" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
        ttr = ttr + "// КЛЮЧИ ДЛЯ ЧАСТНЫХ ПРИСВОЕНИЙ (start)" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
     
       For ikl = 42 To 59
       
       ' присвоение и проверка данных
       osh_k = 0
       im_k = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(ikl, 147).Value
       ma_k = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(ikl, 150).Value
       mi_k = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(ikl, 151).Value
       kd_k = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(ikl, 152).Value
       
        If Len(im_k) * Len(ma_k) * Len(mi_k) * Len(kd_k) > 0 Then
            If IsNumeric(im_k) = True Then osh_k = osh_k + 1
            If IsNumeric(ma_k) = False Then osh_k = osh_k + 1
            If IsNumeric(mi_k) = False Then osh_k = osh_k + 1
            If IsNumeric(kd_k) = False Then osh_k = osh_k + 1
            If osh = 0 Then
            If kd_k < 0.01 Then osh_k = osh_k + 1
            If mi_k > ma_k Then osh_k = osh_k + 1
            End If
            
            If osh_k > 0 Then
            MsgBox ("В блоке задания ключей в строке " + Str(ikl) + " ошибка данных для переменной <" + im_k + ">" + Chr(13) + Chr(10) + "В ключ LOG переменная не обавлена. Исправьте ошибку при следующей генерации!")
            Else
            ttr = ttr + KLUCH_V_LOG2((im_k), (ma_k), (mi_k), (kd_k)) + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
            ttr = ttr + "//" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
            End If
        End If
       Next ikl
       
Worksheets("CODE").Cells(6, 4).Value = ttr
End Sub

Function make_BU_log(nomBU As Single, ima_z As String) ' создание лога для BU
i = nomBU
make_BU_log = ""
make_BU_log = make_BU_log + "if " + Replace("LOG0[ai]='" + Str(i - 62), " ", "") + "' then LOG0[ai]:=" + Replace("'" + ima_z + "';", " ", "") + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
make_BU_log = make_BU_log + "if (LOG0[ai]='" + ima_z + "') then" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
make_BU_log = make_BU_log + "begin" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
  ''''''' содержание лога при попадании имени БИ (старт)
      tip_u = "'U1_6: ('+"
                For iu = 1 To 6
                tip_u = tip_u + " cStrF(" + Replace("U" + Str(iu) + ima_z, " ", "") + ",2)+'/'+"
                Next iu
make_BU_log = make_BU_log + "CURRENT_I_NAME:='" + ima_z + "';" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
make_BU_log = make_BU_log + "Log('==> БУИ. #" + Str(i - 62) + "_" + ima_z + "='+cStrF(" + ima_z + ",2)+' ати.  '+" + Replace(tip_u + "')'+ ", "/)", ")") + "' Tc='+cStrF(" + Replace("Tc" + ima_z, " ", "") + ",2)+' *C'+" + "' Em='+cStrF(" + Replace("Em" + ima_z, " ", "") + ",2)+" + "' ro='+cStrF(" + Replace("ro" + ima_z, " ", "") + ",3)+' кг/л'" + ");" '+ "  kerr:= -400;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
''''''' содержание лога при попадании имени БИ (стоп)
make_BU_log = make_BU_log + "end;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)

End Function

Function make_TO_log(nom_i As Single) ' создание лога для TO
i = nom_i
                        nn = 67
                        nk = 167
                        ni = 1
                        n_p1 = 37
                        n_p2 = 38
                        n_p3 = 41
                        n_p4 = 42
                        n_p5 = 1
make_TO_log = ""

            ima_z = Replace("exc_" + Str(i - (nn - 1)), " ", "")
            ima_p1 = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, n_p1).Value
            ima_p2 = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, n_p2).Value
            ima_p3 = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, n_p3).Value
            ima_p4 = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, n_p4).Value ' имя регулятора
            ima_p5 = Worksheets("CODE").Cells(i, n_p5).Value

If Len(ima_p1) * Len(ima_p2) > 2 Then
make_TO_log = make_TO_log + "XX0[7]:=0;" + " if (LOG0[ai]='exc_All') then XX0[7]:=1;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
make_TO_log = make_TO_log + "if (LOG0[ai]='exc_All') then LOG0[ai]:=" + Replace("'" + ima_z + "';", " ", "") + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
make_TO_log = make_TO_log + "if " + Replace("LOG0[ai]='exc_" + Str(i - (nn - 1)), " ", "") + "' then LOG0[ai]:=" + Replace("'" + ima_z + "';", " ", "") + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
make_TO_log = make_TO_log + "if " + Replace("LOG0[ai]='exc_" + ima_p1, " ", "") + "' then LOG0[ai]:=" + Replace("'" + ima_z + "';", " ", "") + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
make_TO_log = make_TO_log + "if " + Replace("LOG0[ai]='exc_" + ima_p2, " ", "") + "' then LOG0[ai]:=" + Replace("'" + ima_z + "';", " ", "") + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
make_TO_log = make_TO_log + "if (LOG0[ai]='" + Replace(ima_z, " ", "") + "') then" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
make_TO_log = make_TO_log + "begin" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                   
                    ima_z = "(-" + ima_p1 + ":" + ima_p2 + "-)"
                        ''''''' содержание лога при попадании имени Т/О (старт)
                        t11 = Replace("Tc_2" + ima_p1, " ", "")
                        t12 = Replace("Tc" + ima_p1, " ", "")
                        f1 = Replace("Fm" + ima_p1, " ", "")
                        t21 = Replace("Tc_2" + ima_p2, " ", "")
                        t22 = Replace("Tc" + ima_p2, " ", "")
                        f2 = Replace("Fm" + ima_p2, " ", "")
                        
                        tip1 = 1
                        If ima_p1 = "V1" Then tip1 = 0
                        If ima_p1 = "V2" Then tip1 = 0
                        tip2 = 1
                        If ima_p2 = "V1" Then tip2 = 0
                        If ima_p2 = "V2" Then tip2 = 0
                        If tip1 = 0 Then
                        t11 = Replace("Tc_vozd", " ", "")
                        t12 = Replace("Tc_vozd", " ", "")
                        f1 = Replace("e27", " ", "")
                        End If
                        
                        If tip2 = 0 Then
                        t21 = Replace("Tc_vozd", " ", "")
                        t22 = Replace("Tc_vozd", " ", "")
                        f2 = Replace("e27", " ", "")
                        End If
                        make_TO_log = make_TO_log + "CURRENT_I_NAME:='" + ima_z + "';" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                        make_TO_log = make_TO_log + "Log('==> exc_[Т/О]. #" + Str(i - (nn - 1)) + "_" + ima_z + "  KF='+cStrF(S_TO[" + Str(i - (nn - 1)) + "]*100*" + Str(ima_p3) + "*" + ima_p4 + ",1)+'; Контур 1: T1(1)='+cStrF(" + t11 + ",2)+'*C; T1(2)='+cStrF(" + t12 + ",2)+'*C; Fc1='+cStrF(" + f1 + ",3)+'т/ч   Контур 2: T2(1)='+cStrF(" + t21 + ",2)+'*C; T2(2)='+cStrF(" + t22 + ",2)+'*C; Fc2='+cStrF(" + f2 + ",3)+'т/ч; REG='+cStrF(" + ima_p4 + ",1)+'ед." + "');" '+ "  kerr:= -600;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                         ''''''' содержание лога при попадании имени Т/О (стоп)
                        make_TO_log = make_TO_log + "end;" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                        make_TO_log = make_TO_log + "if (XX0[7]=1) then LOG0[ai]:='exc_All';" + Chr(13) + Chr(10)
                        End If
           
End Function

Function INSERT_Kerr(txt As String, kerr As Single)
INSERT_Kerr = txt + "  " + "kerr:=" + Str(kerr) + ";"
fl = InStr(txt, "//")
If fl > 0 Then
txt1 = Left(txt, fl - 1)
txt2 = Right(txt, Len(txt) - fl + 1)
INSERT_Kerr = txt1 + "  " + "kerr:=" + Str(kerr) + " ;" + txt2
End If

End Function

Function Str2(NOM_to_add_zerro As Single)
Str2 = ""
If Abs(NOM_to_add_zerro) > 1 Then
Str2 = Str(Abs(NOM_to_add_zerro))
Else
Str2 = Replace("0" + Str(Abs(NOM_to_add_zerro)), " ", "")
End If
If NOM_to_add_zerro < 0 Then
Str2 = Replace("-" + Str2, " ", "")
End If
End Function
